Mobile SSH — Shadowsocks third-party notices

Shadowsocks core 1.25.0 and Rust 1.94.0 standard library/runtime.
Generated from the locked normal-dependency closure for all four Android ABIs.
Build-only and dev-only edges are excluded. Normal proc-macro dependencies
and Cargo's shared resolved features are included conservatively. This list
does not imply that every source file survives release linking.

Where MIT OR Apache-2.0 is offered, redistribution uses the MIT option;
other permissive alternatives and additional notices remain reproduced.
The complete Rust upstream notice also lists other-platform components
such as Fortanix SGX and UEFI, which are not Android dependencies.
Their inclusion in that upstream notice does not mean they are bundled.

Identical license texts are reproduced once with all source attributions.

Wycheproof Test Vectors
------------------------
Project Wycheproof is a community managed repository of test vectors that can be used by cryptography library
developers to test against known attacks, specification inconsistencies, and other various implementation bugs.

See third_party/wycheproof_testvectors/LICENSE.

```
SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
```
